- **imprint**: Imprint creates a portable collaboration profile by learning how you work through natural conversation. On first run, it asks a few casual questions about your background, tools, and preferences. From there, it silently observes your behavior patterns across sessions and encodes them into a compact structured file (.dna.md) under 500 tokens.
